Kết quả chấm bài

Substantiated behaviors: 1. Sample DL.exe (SHA-256 5D0E7A13C6B94F28E1A07D3B92C5F6084E1D7B3A60C9F25E8D41B7A3C02F9E64) with execution timeline (12:29-12:33). 2. DNS resolution of update.labnet.local to 10.40.0.10. 3. HTTP GET /files/upd.bin from INetSim. 4. Scheduled task creation for SystemHealthCheck. 5. File write to C:\Users\student\AppData\Roaming\SysHelper\syshelper.exe. Unique defects: 1. Missing hash in chunk_7 (E01). 2. Unresolved evidence reference in chunk_6 (E02). 3. Placeholder in required field (PID in chunk_3). 4. Materially coarse timeline (chunk_3 lacks timestamps). 5. Privilege/result misread (chunk_5 claims stage-1 downloader without evidence). 6. System/tool noise attributed to malware (chunk_2 mentions INetSim default binary as payload). 7. Internal contradiction (chunk_5 claims stage-1 but chunk_3 shows payload execution).
